Let f nonnegative ∫=1. Minimize M. At optimum, active set A where g(t)=M. Introduce dual measure ν on A. Variation f+εh, ∫h=0, f+εh≥0. directional derivative at active t: ∫ h(x)(f(t-x)+f(x-t? same? g=f*f, derivative = ∫ h(x)f(t-x)dx (since only one variable changes? Let's refine full coordinate descent function:
```
def coordinate_polish(p, deadline, rng, grid, refine, cycles_limit=None, coord_per_cycle=0):
    p = normalize; c=np.convolve(p,p); score=score
    best_p=... best_score
    while time<deadline:
       if coord_per_cycle == n: order range(n) else sample without replacement.
       for i in order:
          if time too close break
          p,c,score,imp = update_coord(...)
       recompute p normalized; c=conv; score=score
       if score < best: update
       if no improvement for many cycles: perturb (add multiplicative noise, random clip) or break.
```

`def _score_fft(a):`
```
a=np.asarray(a,float); if size=0 return inf
a=np.clip(a,0,None); s=a.sum(); if s<=0 return inf
p=a/s
n=p.size
fftn=2*n-1
spec=np.fft.rfft(p, fftn)
conv=np.fft.irfft(spec*spec, fftn)
np.maximum(conv,0,out=conv)
m=conv.max()
if not finite return inf
return 2*n*m
```
Maybe for n very small, okay.

Let's detail functions.

```
def _clean_array(arr):
    try:
       a=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
       return np.ones(1)
    if a.ndim!=1: a=a.ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a,0.0)
    np.clip(a,0,1000,out=a)
    return a
```

`_normalize_array(a)`:
```
a=_clean_array(a)
s=a.sum()
if s<=0 or !finite: return np.ones(a.size)/a.size
return a/s
```

`_score_fft(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a)
if a.size==0: inf
a=np.clip(a,0,None)
s=a.sum()
if s<=1e-300: inf
p=a/s
n=p.size
if n==1: return 2.0? conv [p^2], sum p=1, score=2*1*1=2.
fftn=2*n-1
spec=np.fft.rfft(p, fftn)
conv=np.fft.irfft(spec*spec, fftn)
if conv.size < fftn: conv=np.fft.irfft(spec*spec, fftn) # no
# due roundoff small negative
maxv = float(np.max(conv))
if maxv<1e-300: # maybe use sum
return float(2*n*max(0,maxv))
```
The conv from FFT length fftn exactly 2n-1. If n large, next fast length not needed. Use `np.fft.irfft(spec*spec, n=fftn)`.
Potential roundoff: conv sum maybe 1. If max negative due to impossible? no.

`_score_array_conv(p,c)`: return 2n max(c) if p sum1.

`_resample`:
```
def _resample(arr, n):
    arr=_clean_array(arr)
    n=max(1,int(n))
    if arr.size<=1:
       val=float(arr[0]) if arr.size else 0.0
       return _normalize_array(np.full(n,val))
    if arr.size==n: return _normalize_array(arr)
    oldx=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,arr.size)
    newx=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    out=np.interp(newx, oldx, arr)
    return _normalize_array(out)
```

`_two_level` as previous.

return H
```

`_seq_exp(params, basis)`:
```
ex=basis.dot(np.asarray(params))
np.clip(ex,-50,50,out=ex)
p=np.exp(ex)
p=np.maximum(p,0)
s=p.sum()
if s<=0: p=np.ones_like(p)/n
else p/=s
return p
```

`_seq_gauss(params, x, K)`:
params length 3K. If K=0? no.
```
mus=params[:K]; sig=exp(params[K:2K]) clip 0.01,5; w=softmax(params[2K:3K])
f=np.zeros_like(x)
for k in range(K):
   z=(x-mus[k])/sig[k]
   f += w[k] * np.exp(-0.5*z*z)
s=f.sum(); if s>0 f/=s else ones
return f
```
Use `np.exp(np.clip(z,-30,30))`? z small. OK.

    s=int(j*n/m); e=int((j+1)*n/m)
    if e>s: p[s:e]=levels[j]
normalize
```

def _optimize_parameters(family, init_params, bounds, n, aux, K, deadline, rng, max_no_improve=6, refine_count=12):
    D=len(init_params)
    params=init_params.copy()
    sc=_score_family(...)
    best_params=params.copy(); best_score=sc
    no_improve=0
    pass_count=0
    while time.monotonic()+0.5 < deadline and no_improve < max_no_improve:
       pass_count+=1
       order=rng.permutation(D)
       improved=False
       for d in order:
           if time.monotonic()+1.0 > deadline: break
           cur=params[d]
           lo,hi=bounds[d]
           cands=_param_candidates(cur,lo,hi)
           # evaluate candidates
           best_cv=cur; best_val=sc
           cands_scores=[]
           for cv in cands:
               if cv==cur: val=sc
               else:
                   params[d]=cv
                   val=_score_family(...)
               cands_scores.append((val,cv))
           cands_scores.sort(key=lambda? no lambda. Use manual find min:
           min_val=inf; min_cv=cur; min_repl=False
           for val,cv in cands_scores: if val<min_val: ...
           # if improvement:
           if min_val < sc - 1e-10:
               # local refine around min_cv maybe using cands values? We can generate local grid based on min_cv and current best neighbors? Simpler generate uniform around min_cv:
               local = _local_candidates(min_cv, lo, hi, refine_count)
               for lv in local:
                  params[d]=lv; val=score
                  if val<min_val: ...
               params[d]=min_cv
               sc=min_val
               improved=True
           else:
               params[d]=cur
               # ensure sc unchanged? might have changed due eval? no
       # end for
       # After pass, recompute sc for safety
       sc=_score_family(family,params,n,aux,K)
       if sc < best_score - 1e-10:
           best_score=sc; best_params=params.copy(); no_improve=0
       else:
           no_improve+=1
           if not improved:
              # small random perturbation if no_improve < max_no_improve
              scale=0.3/(no_improve)
              pert=params + rng.normal(0,scale,D)
              # clip to bounds
              for j in range(D): pert[j]=float(np.clip(pert[j], bounds[j][0], bounds[j][1]))
              pval=_score_family(...)
              if pval < best_score - 1e-10:
                  params=pert.copy(); best_params=...; best_score=pval; sc=pval; no_improve=0
              elif pval < sc -1e-10:
                  params=pert.copy(); sc=pval; no_improve=0
              else:
                  # restore best?
                  params=best_params.copy()
       # avoid too many passes: if time? ok
    return best_params, best_score
```

Subgradient best response via multiplicative weights:
Given p,c. Choose top lags K (e.g., 5). We want reduce them. Compute `v = zeros(n)`. For each lag k in top indices, `v[i] += p[k-i]` where valid (shift). Since derivative is 2 shift, we can sum. Then for a few beta values, q = p / (1 + beta*v); normalize; evaluate score. Also try q = (1-t)p + t * project(p - eta*v) for eta. If improves, accept. This can move many coords. Implement in `_subgrad_attempt(p, c, score, rng)`.

Need fast compute v. For each k in top lags, valid i from max(0,k-n+1) to min(n-1,k). Let j=k-i. Then i = k-j. We can add `p[j]` to `v[i]`. This is a slice of p reversed: for i range l..r, j=k-i decreasing. We can vectorize: `v[l:r+1] += p[k-r:k-l+1][::-1]`. Good. Top lags using `np.argpartition(c, top)`? c length 2n-1; top=5. `idx=np.argsort(c)[-top:]` O(n log n) okay occasionally. Or `np.argpartition` for top. ```
def _subgradient_attempt(p,c,score,rng):
    best improves
    n=p.size
    for attempt in range(4):
       top_idx=np.argsort(c)[-3:] # K=3
       v=np.zeros(n)

Pseudo update:
```
def _coordinate_update_fast(p,c,score,i,grid,refine_count):
    n=p.size; old=float(p[i]); D=1.0-old
    if old<0: old=0
    ks=slice(i,i+n)
    c_k=c[ks]
    base=c_k - 2.0*old*p
    mid_c=float(c[2*i])
    end=i+n
    max_unaff=0.0
    if i>0: max_unaff=max(max_unaff,float(np.max(c[:i])))
    if end<2*n-1: max_unaff=max(max_unaff,float(np.max(c[end:])))
    # initial xs
    xs = grid.copy()
    if old > xs.max(): xs=np.concatenate((xs, old*np.array([0.1,0.2,0.5,2,5,10])))
    xs.tolist += old? We can append old separately.
    xs = xs[np.isfinite&(xs>=0)] maybe copy.
    aff = base + 2.0*xs[:,None]*p
    aff[:, i] = mid_c - old*old + xs*xs
    max_aff=np.max(aff,axis=1)
    maxc=np.maximum(max_unaff, max_aff)
    S=D+xs
    vals=np.full(...)
    valid=S>1e-12
    vals[valid]=(2*n)*maxc[valid]/(S[valid]*S[valid])
    valid_idx = np.where(~np.isfinite(vals))[0] set inf
    idx=np.argmin(vals); best_x=xs[idx]; best_val=vals[idx]
    # refine
    if refine_count>0 and np.isfinite(best_val):
       if best_x > 0:
          local = best_x * np.array([0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,2,5,10])[:refine_count?]
       else:
          # small positive up to maybe old or 1e-3
          upper = max(1e-6, old, float(np.max(grid))*0.1?)
          local = np.logspace(-12, np.log10(max(upper,1e-12)), int(refine_count))
       local = np.unique(np.concatenate((local,[0,best_x,old])))
       # evaluate same
       ...
       if better: best
    if not finite or best_val >= score - tol: return no improvement
    S=D+best_x; S2=S*S
    aff_best = base + 2*best_x*p
    aff_best[i]=mid_c - old*old + best_x*best_x
    c_new=c/S2
    c_new[ks]=aff_best/S2
    p_new=p/S
    p_new[i]=best_x/S
    # clean
    np.clip(p_new,0,None,out=p_new); np.clip(c_new,0,None,out=c_new)
    snew=p_new.sum()
    if snew>0: p_new/=snew; c_new/=snew*snew
    score_new=2*n*float(np.max(c_new))
    if not finite or score_new >= score - tol: return no
    return p_new,c_new,score_new,True
```

Subgradient attempt top-level:
```
def _subgradient_attempt(p, c, score, rng, attempts=3):
    n=p.size; best_p=p.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_score=score; improved=False
    for _ in range(int(attempts)):
        if c.size == 0: break
        # top 3 lags
        top = min(3,c.size)
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -top)[-top:] if c.size>top else np.arange(c.size)
        v=np.zeros(n)
        for k in idx:
            l=max(0, int(k)-n+1); r=min(n-1, int(k))
            if r>=l:
                v[l:r+1] += p[int(k)-r:int(k)-l+1][::-1]
        vmax=float(np.max(v))
        if not finite or vmax<=0: continue
        scale=max(1e-12, float(n)*vmax)
        for raw in (0.1,0.5,2.0,10.0):
            beta=raw/scale
            q = p / (1.0 + beta*v)
            s=q.sum(); if s<=0 continue
            q/=s
            sc = _score_fft(q)
            if finite and sc < best_score -1e-11:
                best=q; best_score=sc; best_c=np.convolve(q,q); improved=True
    return best_p,best_c,best_score,improved
```
`np.argpartition(c, -top)` if top=c.size? use argsort. c length ~7617; okay. We may not need subgrad in final due time; call only stagnation.

```

`_score_fft` uses `_fft_len`.

`_fft_len`:
```
def _fft_len(n):
    m=2*int(n)-1
    if m<=1: return 1
    return 1 << ((m-1).bit_length())
```
This gives next power of two >=m. For m=7617, bit_length of 7616? (7616).bit_length=13 =>8192. Good.

def _evaluate_external(seq):
    try:
        arr=np.asarray(seq,float).ravel()
        vals=[float(x) for x in arr]
        val=float(evaluate_sequence(vals))
        if math.isfinite(val): return val
    except Exception:
        pass
    return _score_fft(seq)
```

Implementation details in `_optimize_parameters` without lambdas:
```
def _optimize_parameters(...):
    D=len(init_params)
    params=init_params.astype(np.float64).copy()
    sc=_score_family(...)
    if not finite: return init_params, inf
    best_params=params.copy(); best_score=sc
    no_improve=0
    max_no_improve=int(max_no_improve)
    while time.monotonic() + 2.0 < deadline and no_improve < max_no_improve:
        order=rng.permutation(D)
        improved=False
        cur_score=sc
        for d in order:
            if time.monotonic() + 2.0 > deadline: break
            cur=params[d]
            lo,hi=bounds[d]
            cands=_param_candidates(cur,lo,hi)
            min_val=cur_score
            min_cv=cur
            for cv in cands:
                if cv == cur: continue
                params[d]=cv
                val=_score_family(...)
                if np.isfinite(val) and val < min_val:
                    min_val=val; min_cv=cv
            if min_val < cur_score - 1e-10:
                # local refine
                radius = hi-lo
                if radius > 1e-12:
                    local = np.linspace(max(lo, min_cv - radius*0.05), min(hi, min_cv + radius*0.05), int(refine_count)+2)
                    for lv in local:
                        params[d]=lv
                        val=_score_family(...)
                        if finite and val<min_val: min_val=val; min_cv=lv
                params[d]=min_cv
                cur_score=min_val
                improved=True
            else:
                params[d]=cur
        # pass
        if improved:
            sc=_score_family(...)
            if sc < best_score - 1e-10:
                best_score=sc; best_params=params.copy()
            if sc >= best_score + 1e-8:
                params=best_params.copy()
                sc=best_score
            no_improve=0
        else:
            no_improve+=1
            # random perturbation
            scale = max(0.05, 1.0/(no_improve+1.0)) * 0.2?
            pert=params.copy()
            for j in range(D):
                pert[j]+=rng.normal(0.0,scale)
                if pert[j]<bounds[j][0]: pert[j]=bounds[j][0]
                elif pert[j]>bounds[j][1]: pert[j]=bounds[j][1]
            pval=_score_family(...)
            if np.isfinite(pval) and pval < best_score - 1e-10:
                params=pert.copy(); best_score=pval; best_params=params.copy(); sc=pval; no_improve=0
            elif np.isfinite(pval) and pval < sc - 1e-10:
                params=pert.copy(); sc=pval; no_improve=0
            else:
                params=best_params.copy(); sc=best_score
                if no_improve >= 3: break? max_no_improve handles
    return best_params,best_score
```

```

```
def _optimize_family_starts(..., topk, total_time, deadline, ...):
    if total_time <=0 or deadline < now: return
    scored=[] ...
    if not scored: return
    scored.sort(key=_scored_key)
    k=min(topk,len(scored))
    per=total_time/float(k)
    for idx in range(k):
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem < 15: break
       slice=min(per+5, rem-5)
       if slice <10: break
       pinit=scored[idx][1]
       pbest,scan=_optimize_parameters(..., deadline=time.monotonic()+slice,...)
       seq=_seq_from_params(...)
       _add_candidate(pool,seq,scan)
```

`_random_proto(m, trial, rng)` strategies top-level:
```
def _random_proto(m, trial, rng):
    st=int(trial)%8
    if st==0: ones
    elif st==1: rng.dirichlet(np.ones(m))
    elif st==2: rng.dirichlet(np.full(m,0.3))
    elif st==3: np.exp(rng.normal(0,1,m))
    elif st==4: np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.5,m))
    elif st==5: _two_level(m)
    elif st==6: x=linspace; p=np.exp(3*x)*... 
    else: binary + noise
    return _normalize_array(p)
```

`_coordinate_polish`: We'll include subgradient. Need `_fft_len`? no.
```
def _coordinate_polish(p, deadline, rng, grid, refine_count, max_perturb=3):
    p=_normalize_array(p).copy()
    n=p.size
    c=np.convolve(p,p); c=np.where(c>0,c,0)
    score=2*n*float(np.max(c))
    best_p=p.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_score=score
    perts=0; no_best=0; passes=0
    while time.monotonic() + 3.0 < deadline:
        passes +=1
        order = rng.permutation(n)
        improved = False
        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0: break
            p_new,c_new,sc_new,imp=_coordinate_update_fast(p,c,score,int(i),grid,refine_count)
            if imp:
                p,c,score=p_new,c_new,sc_new; improved=True
        # recompute
        s=p.sum(); if s<=0 or not finite: break
        p/=s
        c=np.convolve(p,p); np.clip(c,0,None,out=c)
        score=2*n*float(np.max(c))
        if score < best_score - 1e-11:
            best_score=score; best_p=p.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); no_best=0
        else:
            no_best +=1
            if score > best_score * 1.1 + 1e-9:
                p=best_p.copy(); c=best_c.copy(); score=best_score
            if improved:
                no_best = min(no_best,1) # maybe
        if not improved:
            # subgrad every few
            if no_best % 2 == 0 and time.monotonic()+10 < deadline:
                p2,c2,sc2,sgimp=_subgradient_attempt(p,c,score,rng, attempts=2)
                if sgimp:
                    p=p2; c=c2; score=sc2
                    if score<best_score-1e-11: best...
                    no_best=0
                    improved=True
            if no_best >= 8 or (not improved and no_best >=4 and perts>=max_perturb): break
        if not improved and perts < max_perturb and time.monotonic()+20 < deadline:
            scales=[0.8,0.5,0.3,0.15]
            scale=scales[min(perts,len-1)]
            p = best_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0,scale,n))
            p=np.clip(p,0,None); s=p.sum(); if s>0:
                p/=s
                c=np.convolve(p,p); clip
                score=2*n*max(c)
                if score<best_score-1e-10: best...
            perts+=1
            no_best=0
        elif not improved and no_best >=6:
            break
        if no_best >=10: break
    return best_p,best_c,best_score
```

Now, code details for `_param_candidates`:
```
def _param_candidates(cur, lo, hi):
    cur = float(cur)
    if not np.isfinite(cur): cur=float((lo+hi)*0.5)
    if cur < lo: cur=lo
    elif cur > hi: cur=hi
    span=hi-lo
    vals=[cur, lo, hi]
    if span > 1e-12:
        mults = np.array([-0.5,-0.3,-0.15,-0.07,-0.03,-0.012,0.012,0.03,0.07,0.15,0.3,0.5])
        vals.extend((cur+span*mults).tolist())
        # additionally some fractional positions
        for q in (0.1,0.25,0.4,0.6,0.75,0.9):
            vals.append(lo+span*q)
    # unique within bounds
    out=[]
    seen=set() maybe cannot use unhashable? floats. Just list and check.
    for v in vals:
       if not finite: continue
       if v < lo - 1e-9 or v > hi +1e-9: continue
       v=float(np.clip(v,lo,hi))
       # avoid duplicates: if any(abs(v-o)<=1e-12...)
       dup=False
       for o in out:
          if abs(v-o) <= 1e-12*max(1.0,abs(v)): dup=True; break
       if not dup: out.append(v)
    return out
```
No set to avoid issues. Duplicates small.

In `_optimize_parameters`, cands length maybe 20. Evaluating all each coordinate many. Good.

def _prototype_search(pool, time_budget, n_target, rng):
   if time_budget < 30: return
   deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, time_budget - 5)
   sizes=[64,128,256]
   trials=3
   total=len(sizes)*trials
   per=(deadline - now)/float(max(1,total))
   for m in sizes:
     for trial in range(trials):
       rem=deadline-now
       if rem<20: return
       slice=min(per+5, rem-5)
       if slice<10: continue
       p=_random_proto(m, int(m+trial), rng)
       grid,ref=_grid_params(m)
       p2,c2,sc2=_coordinate_polish(p, now+slice, rng, grid, ref, max_perturb=2, coord_count=None)
       _add_candidate(pool,p2,sc2)
       if m != n_target:
          p3=_resample(p2,n_target); sc3=_score_fft(p3); _add_candidate(pool,p3,sc3)
```

`_make_exp_inits`: If D=8, random coefficients. Add phase inits for sin/cos. Since basis order: 1:c,2:c²,3:c³,4:sin2,5:cos2-0.5,6:sin4,7:cos4-0.5,8:cos6-0.5. For sine phase, set p[3]=a*cosphi? p[4]=a*sinphi? The linear combination of sin and cos-0.5 includes -0.5a sin? The constant offset in basis cos-0.5 means if p only cos, adds -p[4]/2. This shifts. Fine. For phase, not exact but random.

`_make_lin_inits`: basis includes constant as index0. Set p[0]=1+...; other random. Good.

Now, include random paired initial? Maybe not needed.

Let's now produce final code. Ensure indentation correct. Use comments concise.

After final, no analysis. Done.
</think>

```python
"""
Search for a low-score nonnegative sequence for

    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

The method combines:
  * seeded global shape search in low-dimensional function families
    (affine/Fourier with clipping, exponential basis, Gaussians, steps);
  * coordinate line-search polish on prototypes;
  * full exact one-coordinate descent using an incremental convolution update;
  * sparse stochastic coordinate cycles and subgradient/reweighting escapes;
  * resampling to many lengths and final external verification when available.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math


def _candidate_key(cand):
    return cand[0]


def _scored_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _clean_array(arr):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0, out=a)
    return a


def _normalize_array(a):
    a = _clean_array(a)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        n = max(1, a.size)
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def _fft_len(n):
    m = 2 * int(n) - 1
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << ((m - 1).bit_length())


def _score_fft(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    np.clip(a, 0.0, None, out=a)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    p = a / s
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 1:
        return 2.0
    fftn = _fft_len(n)
    spec = np.fft.rfft(p, fftn)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(spec * spec, fftn)
    conv = conv[:2 * n - 1]
    conv = np.where(np.isfinite(conv) & (conv > 0.0), conv, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(conv))
    if not math.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * mx)


def _score_normalized_convolution(p, c):
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, mx))


def _two_level(n):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    m = n // 2
    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p[:m] = (2.0 / 3.0) / float(m)
    p[m:] = (1.0 / 3.0) / float(n - m)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s > 0:
        p /= s
    return p


def _resample(arr, n):
    arr = _clean_array(arr)
    n = max(1, int(n))
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if arr.size == 1:
        val = float(arr[0])
        if val <= 0:
            val = 1.0
        return _normalize_array(np.full(n, val, dtype=np.float64))
    if arr.size == n:
        return _normalize_array(arr)
    oldx = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    newx = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.interp(newx, oldx, arr)
    return _normalize_array(out)


def _make_basis(n, with_const):
    n = int(n)
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    c = x - 0.5
    rows = []
    if with_const:
        rows.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    c2 = c * c
    c3 = c2 * c
    rows.append(c)
    rows.append(c2 - 1.0 / 12.0)
    rows.append(c3)
    rows.append(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x))
    rows.append(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x) - 0.5)
    rows.append(np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x))
    rows.append(np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x) - 0.5)
    rows.append(np.cos(6.0 * np.pi * x) - 0.5)
    return np.vstack(rows).astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def _seq_exp(params, basis):
    ex = basis.dot(np.asarray(params, dtype=np.float64))
    np.clip(ex, -30.0, 30.0, out=ex)
    p = np.exp(ex)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p) & (p > 0.0), p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        n = p.size
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return p / s


def _seq_lin(params, basis):
    vals = basis.dot(np.asarray(params, dtype=np.float64))
    vals = np.where(np.isfinite(vals), vals, 0.0)
    np.clip(vals, 0.0, None, out=vals)
    s = float(np.sum(vals))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        n = vals.size
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return vals / s


def _softmax(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, -30.0)
    v -= float(np.max(v))
    e = np.exp(v)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0:
        return np.full(e.size, 1.0 / e.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _seq_gauss(params, x, K):
    K = int(K)
    if K <= 0:
        return np.ones(x.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, x.size))
    params = np.asarray(params, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    mu = params[:K]
    log_sig = params[K:2 * K]
    log_w = params[2 * K:3 * K]
    mu = np.clip(mu, 0.0, 1.0)
    sig = np.exp(np.clip(log_sig, -4.6, 3.0))
    sig = np.clip(sig, 0.01, 10.0)
    w = _softmax(log_w)
    f = np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(K):
        if w[k] <= 0:
            continue
        z = (x - mu[k]) / sig[k]
        z = np.clip(z, -40.0, 40.0)
        f += w[k] * np.exp(-0.5 * z * z)
    s = float(np.sum(f))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(x.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, x.size))
    return f / s


def _seq_step(params, n, m):
    n = int(n)
    m = max(1, int(m))
    params = np.asarray(params, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if params.size < m:
        full = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        full[:params.size] = params
        p = full
    else:
        p = params[:m]
    np.clip(p, -50.0, 50.0, out=p)
    levels = np.exp(p)
    levels = np.where(np.isfinite(levels) & (levels > 0.0), levels, 0.0)
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(m):
        a = int(j * n // m)
        b = int((j + 1) * n // m)
        if b > a:
            out[a:b] = levels[j]
    s = float(np.sum(out))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return out / s


def _seq_from_params(family, params, n, aux, K):
    try:
        if family == "exp":
            return _seq_exp(params, aux)
        if family == "lin":
            return _seq_lin(params, aux)
        if family == "gauss":
            return _seq_gauss(params, aux, K)
        if family == "step":
            return _seq_step(params, n, K)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return np.ones(max(1, int(n)), dtype=np.float64)


def _score_family(family, params, n, aux, K):
    try:
        seq = _seq_from_params(family, params, n, aux, K)
        sc = _score_fft(seq)
        if math.isfinite(sc):
            return float(sc)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return float("inf")


def _param_candidates(cur, lo, hi):
    cur = float(cur)
    if not math.isfinite(cur):
        cur = 0.5 * (float(lo) + float(hi))
    cur = float(np.clip(cur, lo, hi))
    lo = float(lo)
    hi = float(hi)
    span = hi - lo
    vals = [cur, lo, hi]
    if span > 1e-12:
        mults = np.array(
            [-0.5, -0.3, -0.15, -0.07, -0.03, -0.012,
             0.012, 0.03, 0.07, 0.15, 0.3, 0.5],
            dtype=np.float64
        )
        vals.extend((cur + span * mults).tolist())
        for q in (0.1, 0.25, 0.4, 0.6, 0.75, 0.9):
            vals.append(lo + span * q)
    out = []
    for v in vals:
        try:
            v = float(v)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if not math.isfinite(v):
            continue
        if v < lo - 1e-9 or v > hi + 1e-9:
            continue
        v = float(np.clip(v, lo, hi))
        dup = False
        for o in out:
            if abs(v - o) <= 1e-12 * max(1.0, abs(v), abs(o)):
                dup = True
                break
        if not dup:
            out.append(v)
    return out


def _optimize_parameters(family, init_params, bounds, n, aux, K, deadline, rng,
                         max_no_improve=7, refine_count=14):
    init_params = np.asarray(init_params, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    D = int(init_params.size)
    if D == 0:
        return init_params, _score_family(family, init_params, n, aux, K)
    params = init_params.copy()
    sc = _score_family(family, params, n, aux, K)
    if not math.isfinite(sc):
        return init_params, float("inf")
    best_params = params.copy()
    best_score = float(sc)
    no_improve = 0
    max_no_improve = max(2, int(max_no_improve))

    while time.monotonic() + 2.0 < float(deadline) and no_improve < max_no_improve:
        order = rng.permutation(D)
        improved = False
        cur_score = float(sc)

        for d in order:
            if time.monotonic() + 2.0 > float(deadline):
                break
            d = int(d)
            cur = float(params[d])
            lo, hi = bounds[d]
            cands = _param_candidates(cur, lo, hi)
            min_val = cur_score
            min_cv = cur

            for cv in cands:
                if cv == cur:
                    continue
                params[d] = cv
                val = _score_family(family, params, n, aux, K)
                if math.isfinite(val) and val < min_val:
                    min_val = float(val)
                    min_cv = float(cv)

            if min_val < cur_score - 1e-11:
                radius = hi - lo
                if radius > 1e-12:
                    a = max(lo, min_cv - 0.06 * radius)
                    b = min(hi, min_cv + 0.06 * radius)
                    if b > a:
                        local = np.linspace(a, b, int(max(3, refine_count)))
                        for lv in local:
                            params[d] = float(lv)
                            val = _score_family(family, params, n, aux, K)
                            if math.isfinite(val) and val < min_val:
                                min_val = float(val)
                                min_cv = float(lv)
                params[d] = min_cv
                cur_score = min_val
                improved = True
            else:
                params[d] = cur

        sc2 = _score_family(family, params, n, aux, K)
        if not math.isfinite(sc2):
            params = best_params.copy()
            sc2 = best_score

        if sc2 < best_score - 1e-11:
            best_score = float(sc2)
            best_params = params.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 3:
                params = best_params.copy()
                sc2 = best_score

        if not improved:
            # Small bounded perturbation, but only in parameter space.
            pert = params.copy()
            for j in range(D):
                lo, hi = bounds[j]
                step = max(0.05, 0.06 * (hi - lo))
                pert[j] += float(rng.normal(0.0, step))
                if pert[j] < lo:
                    pert[j] = lo
                elif pert[j] > hi:
                    pert[j] = hi
            pval = _score_family(family, pert, n, aux, K)
            if math.isfinite(pval):
                if pval < best_score - 1e-11:
                    best_score = float(pval)
                    best_params = pert.copy()
                    params = pert.copy()
                    sc2 = float(pval)
                    no_improve = 0
                elif pval < sc2 - 1e-11:
                    params = pert.copy()
                    sc2 = float(pval)
                    no_improve = 0
                else:
                    params = best_params.copy()
                    sc2 = best_score

    return best_params, best_score


def _optimize_family_starts(family, init_params, bounds, n, aux, K, topk,
                            total_time, deadline, rng, pool):
    total_time = float(max(0.0, total_time))
    deadline = float(max(0.0, deadline))
    if total_time <= 5.0 or time.monotonic() >= deadline - 10.0:
        return

    scored = []
    for p in init_params:
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        if p.size == len(bounds):
            sc = _score_family(family, p, n, aux, K)
            if math.isfinite(sc):
                scored.append([sc, p])

    if not scored:
        return
    scored.sort(key=_scored_key)
    k = max(1, int(min(topk, len(scored))))
    per = total_time / float(k)

    for idx in range(k):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 20.0:
            break
        sl = min(per + 5.0, rem - 5.0)
        if sl < 12.0:
            break
        pinit = scored[idx][1]
        best_p, best_sc = _optimize_parameters(
            family, pinit, bounds, n, aux, K,
            time.monotonic() + sl, rng,
            max_no_improve=7, refine_count=12
        )
        if math.isfinite(best_sc):
            seq = _seq_from_params(family, best_p, n, aux, K)
            _add_candidate(pool, seq, best_sc)


def _make_exp_inits(rng, D):
    inits = []
    scales = [0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0, 12.0]
    for _ in range(90):
        s = float(rng.choice(scales))
        p = rng.normal(0.0, s, D)
        inits.append(p)
    for a in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0):
        p = np.zeros(D)
        p[0] = a
        inits.append(p)
        p = np.zeros(D)
        p[0] = -a
        inits.append(p)
    phs = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, 12, endpoint=False)
    for a in (0.5, 1.5, 3.0, 6.0):
        for ph in phs:
            p = np.zeros(D)
            if D > 3:
                p[3] = a * float(np.cos(ph))
            if D > 4:
                p[4] = a * float(np.sin(ph))
            inits.append(p)
    return inits


def _make_lin_inits(rng, D):
    inits = []
    scales = [0.3, 0.8, 1.5, 3.0, 5.0, 8.0]
    for _ in range(90):
        p = rng.normal(0.0, float(rng.choice(scales)), D)
        p[0] = 1.0 + 0.5 * float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0))
        inits.append(p)
    for a in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0, 14.0):
        p = np.full(D, 0.0)
        p[0] = 1.0
        p[1] = a
        inits.append(p)
        p[1] = -a
        inits.append(p)
    phs = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, 12, endpoint=False)
    for a in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        for ph in phs:
            p = np.full(D, 0.0)
            p[0] = 1.0
            if D > 4:
                p[4] = a * float(np.cos(ph))
            if D > 5:
                p[5] = -a * float(np.sin(ph))
            inits.append(p)
    return inits


def _gauss_bounds(K):
    K = int(K)
    b = []
    for _ in range(K):
        b.append((0.0, 1.0))
    for _ in range(K):
        b.append((-4.6, 3.0))
    for _ in range(K):
        b.append((-6.0, 6.0))
    return b


def _make_gauss_inits(rng, K):
    K = int(K)
    inits = []
    for _ in range(80):
        mu = np.sort(rng.random(K))
        if rng.random() < 0.2:
            mu = np.concatenate((mu, [0.0]))[0:K]
        if rng.random() < 0.2:
            mu = np.concatenate((mu, [1.0]))[0:K]
        logsig = rng.normal(-1.2, 0.75, K)
        logsig = np.clip(logsig, -4.2, 2.0)
        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            w = rng.dirichlet(np.full(K, 0.35))
        else:
            w = rng.dirichlet(np.full(K, 1.4))
        w = np.maximum(w, 1e-6)
        logw = np.log(w)
        p = np.concatenate((mu, logsig, logw))
        inits.append(p)

    for (m1, m2, w1, w2, s1, s2) in [
        (0.25, 0.75, 0.55, 0.45, 0.25, 0.30),
        (0.20, 0.80, 0.40, 0.60, 0.28, 0.24),
        (0.35, 0.65, 0.55, 0.45, 0.22, 0.35),
        (0.15, 0.85, 0.35, 0.65, 0.22, 0.30),
        (0.30, 0.70, 0.65, 0.35, 0.25, 0.25),
    ]:
        if K >= 2:
            if w1 > w2:
                mu = np.array([m1, m2])
                w = np.array([w1, w2])
                sig = np.array([s1, s2])
            else:
                mu = np.array([m2, m1])
                w = np.array([w2, w1])
                sig = np.array([s2, s1])
            if K == 3:
                mu = np.array([min(mu[0], mu[1]), 0.5, max(mu[0], mu[1])])
                sig = np.array([sig[0], 0.30, sig[1]])
                w = np.array([w[0] * 0.5, 0.0, w[1] * 0.5])
            w = np.maximum(w, 1e-6)
            w /= w.sum()
            p = np.concatenate((mu[:K], np.log(sig[:K]), np.log(w[:K])))
            inits.append(p)
    return inits[:180]


def _make_step_inits(rng, m):
    inits = []
    for _ in range(50):
        alpha = float(rng.choice([0.2, 0.4, 0.8, 1.5, 3.0]))
        w = rng.dirichlet(np.full(m, alpha))
        w = np.maximum(w, 1e-6)
        inits.append(np.log(w))
    return inits


def _random_proto(m, state, rng):
    m = max(2, int(m))
    st = int(state) % 10
    if st == 0:
        p = np.ones(m)
    elif st == 1:
        p = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(m))
    elif st == 2:
        p = rng.dirichlet(np.full(m, 0.4))
    elif st == 3:
        p = rng.dirichlet(np.full(m, 0.15))
    elif st == 4:
        p = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, m))
    elif st == 5:
        p = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.4, m))
    elif st == 6:
        p = _two_level(m)
    elif st == 7:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
        p = np.exp(2.0 * x) * (1.0 + 0.1 * rng.random(m))
    elif st == 8:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
        p = np.exp(-2.0 * x) * (1.0 + 0.1 * rng.random(m))
    else:
        t = (np.arange(m) - m / 2.0) / (m / 2.0)
        p = 1.0 + 0.7 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x + float(rng.uniform(0.0, 6.28))) if False else np.maximum(0.1, 1.0 + 0.8 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t + float(rng.uniform(0.0, 6.28))))
    return _normalize_array(p)


def _add_candidate(pool, seq, score, limit=28):
    try:
        score = float(score)
    except Exception:
        return
    if not math.isfinite(score) or score <= 0.0:
        return
    seq = _normalize_array(seq)
    if seq.size == 0:
        seq = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    pool.append([score, int(seq.size), seq.copy()])
    pool.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    while len(pool) > limit:
        pool.pop()


def _grid_params(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 16:
        return 80, 18
    if n <= 32:
        return 64, 15
    if n <= 64:
        return 50, 12
    if n <= 128:
        return 38, 9
    if n <= 256:
        return 28, 7
    if n <= 1024:
        return 20, 5
    return 15, 4


def _grid_array(grid_count):
    g = np.logspace(-9.0, 2.0, int(max(3, grid_count)))
    g = np.concatenate(([0.0], g))
    return g


def _evaluate_coordinate_values(p, c, i, base, mid_c, max_unaff, xs):
    n = p.size
    aff = base + 2.0 * xs[:, None] * p
    aff[:, i] = mid_c + xs * xs
    max_aff = np.max(aff, axis=1)
    maxc = np.maximum(max_unaff, max_aff)
    S = xs  # passed D+xs by caller
    vals = np.full(xs.size, float("inf"), dtype=np.float64)
    valid = S > 1e-14
    vals[valid] = (2.0 * n) * maxc[valid] / (S[valid] * S[valid])
    finite = np.where(np.isfinite(vals))[0]
    if finite.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), 0.0
    idx = int(finite[np.argmin(vals[finite])])
    return float(vals[idx]), float(xs[idx])


def _coordinate_update_fast(p, c, score, i, grid, refine_count):
    n = int(p.size)
    i = int(i)
    if n <= 1:
        return p, c, score, False
    old = float(p[i])
    if not math.isfinite(old) or old < 0.0:
        old = 0.0
    D = 1.0 - old
    if D < 0.0:
        D = 0.0

    ks = slice(i, i + n)
    c_k = c[ks]
    base = c_k - 2.0 * old * p
    mid_c = float(c[2 * i])

    end = i + n
    max_unaff = 0.0
    if i > 0:
        v = float(np.max(c[:i]))
        if math.isfinite(v) and v > max_unaff:
            max_unaff = v
    if end < 2 * n - 1:
        v = float(np.max(c[end:]))
        if math.isfinite(v) and v > max_unaff:
            max_unaff = v

    xs = grid.copy()
    if xs.size == 0:
        return p, c, score, False
    if old > float(np.max(xs)):
        xs = np.concatenate((xs, old * np.array([0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 2.0, 5.0, 10.0])))
    xs = np.concatenate((xs, [old]))
    xs = xs[np.isfinite(xs) & (xs >= 0.0)]
    if xs.size == 0:
        return p, c, score, False

    S = D + xs
    best_val, best_x = _evaluate_coordinate_values(p, c, i, base, mid_c - old * old, max_unaff, S)
    if not math.isfinite(best_val):
        return p, c, score, False

    if int(refine_count) > 0:
        refine_count = int(refine_count)
        if best_x > 1e-12:
            ratios = np.array([0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 2.5, 5.0, 10.0], dtype=np.float64)
            local = best_x * ratios[:min(refine_count, ratios.size)]
        else:
            upper = max(1e-8, old, 0.25 * float(np.max(grid)) if grid.size else 1e-4)
            if upper <= 1e-12:
                upper = 1e-8
            local = np.logspace(-12.0, math.log10(upper), int(max(3, refine_count)))
        local = np.concatenate((local, [0.0, best_x, old]))
        local = local[np.isfinite(local) & (local >= 0.0)]
        local = np.unique(local)
        if local.size > 0:
            S2 = D + local
            l_val, l_x = _evaluate_coordinate_values(p, c, i, base, mid_c - old * old, max_unaff, S2)
            if math.isfinite(l_val) and l_val < best_val:
                best_val = l_val
                best_x = l_x

    tol = 1e-12 * max(1.0, float(score))
    if not math.isfinite(best_val) or best_val >= float(score) - tol:
        return p, c, score, False

    S = D + best_x
    if S <= 1e-14:
        return p, c, score, False
    S2 = S * S

    aff_best = base + 2.0 * best_x * p
    aff_best[i] = mid_c + best_x * best_x - old * old

    c_new = c / S2
    c_new[ks] = aff_best / S2
    p_new = p / S
    p_new[i] = best_x / S

    np.clip(p_new, 0.0, None, out=p_new)
    np.clip(c_new, 0.0, None, out=c_new)

    snew = float(np.sum(p_new))
    if not math.isfinite(snew) or snew <= 1e-14:
        return p, c, score, False
    p_new = p_new / snew
    c_new = c_new / (snew * snew)

    score_new = _score_normalized_convolution(p_new, c_new)
    if not math.isfinite(score_new) or score_new >= float(score) - tol:
        return p, c, score, False
    return p_new, c_new, score_new, True


def _subgradient_attempt(p, c, score, rng, attempts=3):
    n = int(p.size)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_score = float(score)
    improved = False
    attempts = max(1, int(attempts))

    for _ in range(attempts):
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        top_k = min(3, int(c.size))
        if c.size <= top_k:
            idx = np.arange(c.size)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, c.size - top_k)[c.size - top_k:]
        v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for kk in idx:
            k = int(kk)
            l = max(0, k - n + 1)
            r = min(n - 1, k)
            if r >= l:
                v[l:r + 1] += p[k - r:k - l + 1][::-1]
        vmax = float(np.max(v))
        if not math.isfinite(vmax) or vmax <= 0.0:
            continue
        scale = max(1e-12, float(n) * vmax)
        for raw in (0.1, 0.5, 2.0, 10.0):
            beta = raw / scale
            q = p / (1.0 + beta * v)
            s = float(np.sum(q))
            if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-14:
                continue
            q = q / s
            sc = _score_fft(q)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-11:
                best_score = float(sc)
                best_p = q.copy()
                best_c = np.convolve(q, q)
                best_c = np.where(best_c > 0.0, best_c, 0.0)
                improved = True
    return best_p, best_c, best_score, improved


def _coordinate_polish(p, deadline, rng, grid, refine_count, max_perturb=3, coord_count=None):
    p = _normalize_array(p).copy()
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 1:
        c = np.convolve(p, p)
        return p, c, _score_normalized_convolution(p, c)

    c = np.convolve(p, p)
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    score = _score_normalized_convolution(p, c)

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_score = float(score)

    perts = 0
    no_best = 0
    noise_scales = [0.9, 0.55, 0.30, 0.15, 0.07]
    deadline = float(deadline)

    while time.monotonic() + 4.0 < deadline:
        if coord_count is None or int(coord_count) >= n or int(coord_count) <= 0:
            order = rng.permutation(n)
        else:
            cc = min(n, int(coord_count))
            order = rng.choice(n, size=cc, replace=False)

        improved = False
        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
                break
            p_new, c_new, sc_new, imp = _coordinate_update_fast(
                p, c, score, int(i), grid, int(refine_count)
            )
            if imp:
                p, c, score = p_new, c_new, sc_new
                improved = True

        s = float(np.sum(p))
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-14:
            break
        p = p / s
        c = np.convolve(p, p)
        c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
        score = _score_normalized_convolution(p, c)
        if not math.isfinite(score):
            break

        tol = 1e-11 * max(1.0, best_score)
        if score < best_score - tol:
            best_score = float(score)
            best_p = p.copy()
            best_c = c.copy()
            no_best = 0
        else:
            no_best += 1
            if no_best >= 3:
                p = best_p.copy()
                c = best_c.copy()
                score = best_score

        if no_best in (2, 4, 6) and time.monotonic() + 15.0 < deadline:
            p2, c2, sc2, sgimproved = _subgradient_attempt(p, c, score, rng, attempts=2)
            if sgimproved and math.isfinite(sc2):
                p, c, score = p2, c2, sc2
                if score < best_score - tol:
                    best_score = float(score)
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    best_c = c.copy()
                no_best = 0
                improved = True

        if not improved:
            if no_best >= 9 or (no_best >= 5 and perts >= max_perturb):
                break
            if perts < max_perturb and time.monotonic() + 30.0 < deadline:
                scale = noise_scales[min(perts, len(noise_scales) - 1)]
                q = best_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, scale, n))
                q = np.where(q > 0.0, q, 0.0)
                qs = float(np.sum(q))
                if math.isfinite(qs) and qs > 1e-14:
                    q = q / qs
                    qc = np.convolve(q, q)
                    qc = np.where(qc > 0.0, qc, 0.0)
                    qsc = _score_normalized_convolution(q, qc)
                    if math.isfinite(qsc) and qsc < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best_score = float(qsc)
                        best_p = q.copy()
                        best_c = qc.copy()
                        p, c, score = q.copy(), qc.copy(), qsc
                        improved = True
                        no_best = 0
                perts += 1
                no_best = 0
            elif no_best >= 6:
                break

    p, c, score = best_p, best_c, best_score
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s > 0 and math.isfinite(s):
        p = p / s
    return p, c, score


def _direction_sweep(pool, n, deadline, rng):
    n = int(n)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)
    phs = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, 16, endpoint=False)
    amps = (0.0, 0.4, 0.8, 1.2, 1.8)

    for amp in amps:
        for ph in phs:
            if time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
                return
            base = 1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x + ph)
            p1 = np.maximum(base, 0.0)
            _add_candidate(pool, p1, _score_fft(p1))
            p2 = np.square(np.clip(1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x + ph), -4.0, 4.0))
            _add_candidate(pool, p2, _score_fft(p2))
            base = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x + ph)
            p3 = np.maximum(base, 0.0)
            _add_candidate(pool, p3, _score_fft(p3))

    for amp in (0.4, 0.8, 1.2):
        for ps in np.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, 8, endpoint=False):
            if time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
                return
            for pb in (0.0, 1.2, 2.0):
                p = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x + pb) + 0.5 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x + ps)
                p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
                _add_candidate(pool, p, _score_fft(p))


def _prototype_search(pool, time_budget, n_target, rng):
    time_budget = float(max(0.0, time_budget))
    if time_budget < 30.0:
        return
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, time_budget - 5.0)
    sizes = [64, 128, 256]
    trials = 3
    total = len(sizes) * trials
    per = max(8.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) / float(max(1, total)))

    for m in sizes:
        for trial in range(trials):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 18.0:
                return
            sl = min(per + 5.0, rem - 5.0)
            if sl < 10.0:
                continue
            p = _random_proto(m, m + trial, rng)
            grid_count, refine_count = _grid_params(m)
            grid = _grid_array(grid_count)
            p2, c2, sc2 = _coordinate_polish(
                p, time.monotonic() + sl, rng, grid, refine_count,
                max_perturb=2, coord_count=None
            )
            _add_candidate(pool, p2, sc2)
            if m != n_target:
                p3 = _resample(p2, n_target)
                _add_candidate(pool, p3, _score_fft(p3))
                p4 = _two_level(n_target)
                _add_candidate(pool, p4, _score_fft(p4))


def _evaluate_external(seq):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        vals = [float(x) for x in arr]
        val = float(evaluate_sequence(vals))
        if math.isfinite(val):
            return val
    except Exception:
        pass
    return _score_fft(seq)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2300, **kwargs):
    """Return a nonnegative sequence with small evaluate_sequence score."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2300.0
    if not math.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 2300.0
    budget = max(30.0, budget)
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, budget - 8.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    n_target = 3809
    pool = []

    base = _two_level(n_target)
    _add_candidate(pool, base, _score_fft(base))

    try:
        hs = _clean_array(height_sequence_1)
        if hs.size > 0:
            sc = _score_fft(hs)
            _add_candidate(pool, hs, sc)
            if hs.size != n_target:
                hs2 = _resample(hs, n_target)
                _add_candidate(pool, hs2, _score_fft(hs2))
    except Exception:
        pass

    # A few analytic sweeps are cheap and can provide very different seeds.
    _direction_sweep(pool, n_target, deadline, rng)

    quick_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if quick_left < 30.0:
        out = _normalize_array(pool[0][2] if pool else _two_level(n_target))
        return [float(x) for x in out]

    lowdim_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + max(220.0, 0.30 * budget))
    proto_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + max(520.0, 0.55 * budget))

    # Low-dimensional shape search on the target length.
    basis_exp = _make_basis(n_target, with_const=False)
    basis_lin = _make_basis(n_target, with_const=True)
    x_target = (np.arange(n_target) + 0.5) / float(n_target)

    rem = lowdim_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 80.0:
        Dexp = basis_exp.shape[0]
        exp_inits = _make_exp_inits(rng, Dexp)
        bounds_exp = [(-10.0, 10.0)] * Dexp
        _optimize_family_starts(
            "exp", exp_inits, bounds_exp, n_target, (basis_exp,), 0,
            topk=4, total_time=rem * 0.34, deadline=lowdim_deadline,
            rng=rng, pool=pool
        )

    rem = lowdim_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 80.0:
        Dlin = basis_lin.shape[0]
        lin_inits = _make_lin_inits(rng, Dlin)
        bounds_lin = [(-10.0, 10.0)] * Dlin
        _optimize_family_starts(
            "lin", lin_inits, bounds_lin, n_target, (basis_lin,), 0,
            topk=4, total_time=rem * 0.36, deadline=lowdim_deadline,
            rng=rng, pool=pool
        )

    rem = lowdim_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 70.0:
        inits2 = _make_gauss_inits(rng, 2)
        bounds2 = _gauss_bounds(2)
        _optimize_family_starts(
            "gauss", inits2, bounds2, n_target, (x_target,), 2,
            topk=4, total_time=rem * 0.34, deadline=lowdim_deadline,
            rng=rng, pool=pool
        )

    rem = lowdim_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 70.0:
        inits3 = _make_gauss_inits(rng, 3)
        bounds3 = _gauss_bounds(3)
        _optimize_family_starts(
            "gauss", inits3, bounds3, n_target, (x_target,), 3,
            topk=3, total_time=rem * 0.42, deadline=lowdim_deadline,
            rng=rng, pool=pool
        )

    rem = lowdim_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 50.0:
        best_step_budget = rem
        for m in (2, 3, 4, 6):
            sl = best_step_budget / 4.0
            if time.monotonic() >= lowdim_deadline - 10.0:
                break
            inits = _make_step_inits(rng, m)
            bounds = [(-12.0, 12.0)] * m
            _optimize_family_starts(
                "step", inits, bounds, n_target, (n_target,), m,
                topk=2, total_time=sl, deadline=lowdim_deadline,
                rng=rng, pool=pool
            )

    # Prototype-scale randomized coordinate search.
    rem = proto_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 60.0:
        _prototype_search(pool, rem, n_target, rng)

    pool.sort(key=_candidate_key)

    # Full-length coordinate polish. Prefer a stochastic subset of coordinates
    # for large n so more passes and escapes fit into the time budget.
    final_deadline = max(time.monotonic() + 20.0, deadline - 15.0)
    if pool:
        fracs = [0.40, 0.30, 0.30]
        starts = []
        for cand in pool[:5]:
            starts.append([cand[0], cand[2].copy()])
        starts.sort(key=_scored_key)

        for idx, cand in enumerate(starts[:3]):
            rem = final_deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 45.0:
                break
            frac = fracs[idx] if idx < len(fracs) else 0.25
            sl = min(frac * (rem + 10.0), rem - 5.0)
            if sl < 30.0:
                break
            seq = cand[1]
            n = int(seq.size)
            grid_count, refine_count = _grid_params(n)
            grid = _grid_array(grid_count)
            coord_count = n if n <= 1024 else max(900, n // 3)
            p2, c2, sc2 = _coordinate_polish(
                seq, time.monotonic() + sl, rng, grid, refine_count,
                max_perturb=3, coord_count=coord_count
            )
            _add_candidate(pool, p2, sc2)

        # Spend any remaining time polishing the current global best.
        rem = final_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 70.0:
            pool.sort(key=_candidate_key)
            seq = pool[0][2].copy()
            n = int(seq.size)
            grid_count, refine_count = _grid_params(n)
            grid = _grid_array(grid_count)
            coord_count = n if n <= 1024 else max(900, n // 3)
            p2, c2, sc2 = _coordinate_polish(
                seq, final_deadline - 5.0, rng, grid, refine_count,
                max_perturb=3, coord_count=coord_count
            )
            _add_candidate(pool, p2, sc2)

    pool.sort(key=_candidate_key)

    best_seq = None
    best_val = float("inf")
    limit = min(8, len(pool))
    for i in range(limit):
        cand = pool[i]
        val = _evaluate_external(cand[2])
        if math.isfinite(val) and val < best_val:
            best_val = float(val)
            best_seq = cand[2]

    if best_seq is None:
        if pool:
            best_seq = pool[0][2]
        else:
            best_seq = _two_level(n_target)

    out = _normalize_array(best_seq)
    if out.size == 0:
        out = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
